## 时空演化路径

### 时间路径

```text
用户说"你好" → 系统处理 → 系统回复"你好！"
    │
    ├── 用户输入节点 (t=0)
    │   spatial_coordinates: {"semantic": [0.2, 0.1, 0.3]}
    │
    ├── TemporalEdge (t=0 → t=0.3, "causal")
    │
    ├── 系统推理节点 (t=0.3)
    │   spatial_coordinates: {"semantic": [0.5, 0.4, 0.2]}
    │
    ├── TemporalEdge (t=0.3 → t=0.5, "causal")
    │
    └── 系统回复节点 (t=0.5)
        spatial_coordinates: {"semantic": [0.8, 0.7, 0.9]}
```

### 空间路径

```text
视觉帧中的"猫"
    │
    ├── 视觉空间坐标: [120, 340, 0.8] (图像坐标+特征)
    │
    ├── SpatialEdge ("contains")
    │
    ├── "猫"的语义坐标: [0.2, 0.5, 0.7] (语义空间)
    │
    ├── SpatialEdge ("connected")
    │
    └── "动物"的语义坐标: [0.1, 0.4, 0.6]
```

---

## 核心操作

### 1. 添加时空节点

```python
def add_spacetime_node(
    self,
    content: Any,
    modality: str = "semantic",
    spatial_coordinates: Dict[str, List[float]] = None,
    temporal_coordinate: float = None,
    parent_node: str = None
) -> SpacetimeNode:
    """添加一个时空节点"""
    node = SpacetimeNode(
        content=content,
        modality=modality,
        spatial_coordinates=spatial_coordinates or {},
        temporal_coordinate=temporal_coordinate or time.time(),
        parent_node=parent_node
    )
    # 存储到图结构
    self.nodes[node.id] = node
    if parent_node and parent_node in self.nodes:
        self.nodes[parent_node].child_nodes.append(node.id)
    return node
```

### 2. 添加时间演化边

```python
def add_temporal_edge(
    self,
    source: str,
    target: str,
    relation_type: str = "sequential",
    confidence: float = 0.5
) -> TemporalEdge:
    """添加时间演化边"""
    # 检查是否已存在相似边，用于累加重复次数
    for edge in self.temporal_edges.values():
        if edge.source == source and edge.target == target:
            edge.repeat_count += 1
            edge.confidence = min(1.0, edge.confidence + 0.05)
            edge.last_observed = time.time()
            return edge
    
    edge = TemporalEdge(
        source=source,
        target=target,
        time_delta=abs(
            self.nodes[target].temporal_coordinate - 
            self.nodes[source].temporal_coordinate
        ),
        relation_type=relation_type,
        confidence=confidence
    )
    self.temporal_edges[edge.id] = edge
    self.nodes[source].temporal_edges.append(edge.id)
    self.nodes[target].temporal_edges.append(edge.id)
    return edge
```

### 3. 时空路径检索

```python
def traverse_spacetime_path(
    self,
    start_id: str,
    mode: str = "temporal",  # temporal | spatial | hybrid
    max_depth: int = 5,
    min_confidence: float = 0.3
) -> List[Dict]:
    """
    时空路径检索
    - temporal: 沿时间轴展开
    - spatial: 沿空间关系展开
    - hybrid: 时间和空间交织展开
    """
    visited = set()
    path = []
    self._traverse_spacetime(start_id, mode, max_depth, min_confidence, visited, path, 0)
    return path

def _traverse_spacetime(self, node_id, mode, max_depth, min_confidence, visited, path, depth):
    if depth > max_depth or node_id in visited:
        return
    visited.add(node_id)
    node = self.nodes.get(node_id)
    if not node:
        return
    
    path.append({
        "node": node,
        "depth": depth,
        "temporal_edges": [self.temporal_edges[eid] for eid in node.temporal_edges if self.temporal_edges[eid].confidence >= min_confidence],
        "spatial_edges": [self.spatial_edges[eid] for eid in node.spatial_edges if self.spatial_edges[eid].confidence >= min_confidence]
    })
    
    if mode in ["temporal", "hybrid"]:
        for edge_id in node.temporal_edges:
            edge = self.temporal_edges.get(edge_id)
            if edge and edge.confidence >= min_confidence:
                next_id = edge.target if edge.source == node_id else edge.source
                self._traverse_spacetime(next_id, mode, max_depth, min_confidence, visited, path, depth + 1)
    
    if mode in ["spatial", "hybrid"]:
        for edge_id in node.spatial_edges:
            edge = self.spatial_edges.get(edge_id)
            if edge and edge.confidence >= min_confidence:
                next_id = edge.target if edge.source == node_id else edge.source
                self._traverse_spacetime(next_id, mode, max_depth, min_confidence, visited, path, depth + 1)
```

### 4. 因果路径识别

```python
def identify_causal_paths(
    self,
    node_id: str,
    min_confidence: float = 0.6,
    min_repeat_count: int = 2
) -> List[List[SpacetimeNode]]:
    """
    识别因果路径
    基于时间演化的重复模式
    """
    causal_paths = []
    node = self.nodes.get(node_id)
    if not node:
        return causal_paths
    
    # 从当前节点出发，沿高置信度、高重复次数的TemporalEdge展开
    for edge_id in node.temporal_edges:
        edge = self.temporal_edges.get(edge_id)
        if not edge:
            continue
        if edge.confidence >= min_confidence and edge.repeat_count >= min_repeat_count:
            path = self._build_causal_path(edge, min_confidence, min_repeat_count)
            if path:
                causal_paths.append(path)
    
    return causal_paths

def _build_causal_path(self, start_edge: TemporalEdge, min_confidence: float, min_repeat_count: int) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
    """构建完整因果链"""
    path = []
    current = start_edge.source
    visited = set()
    
    while current and current not in visited:
        visited.add(current)
        node = self.nodes.get(current)
        if node:
            path.append(node)
        
        # 寻找下一个高置信度边
        next_edge = None
        for edge_id in node.temporal_edges:
            edge = self.temporal_edges.get(edge_id)
            if edge and edge.confidence >= min_confidence and edge.repeat_count >= min_repeat_count:
                if edge.source == current:
                    next_edge = edge
                    current = edge.target
                    break
                elif edge.target == current:
                    # 反向因果
                    pass
        
        if not next_edge:
            break
    
    return path
```

## 与现有记忆系统的集成

```python
class SpacetimeUnifiedMemory:
    """
    时空大一统记忆系统
    整合了五层记忆 + 图结构 + 时空演化
    """
    
    def __init__(self, data_dir: str = "./data"):
        self.data_dir = Path(data_dir)
        self.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        # 时空节点和边
        self.nodes: Dict[str, SpacetimeNode] = {}
        self.temporal_edges: Dict[str, TemporalEdge] = {}
        self.spatial_edges: Dict[str, SpatialEdge] = {}
        
        # 兼容旧五层
        self._anchor = []
        self._self = []
        self._structure = []
        self._context = []
        self._hyperlinks = []
        
        self._load()
    
    # ========== 多模态输入 ==========
    
    def ingest_frame(self, frame_data: Dict) -> SpacetimeNode:
        """摄入视觉帧"""
        return self.add_spacetime_node(
            content=frame_data.get("content"),
            modality="visual",
            spatial_coordinates={"visual": frame_data.get("coordinates", [])},
            temporal_coordinate=frame_data.get("timestamp")
        )
    
    def ingest_audio(self, audio_data: Dict) -> SpacetimeNode:
        """摄入音频"""
        return self.add_spacetime_node(
            content=audio_data.get("content"),
            modality="audio",
            spatial_coordinates={"audio": audio_data.get("frequency", [])},
            temporal_coordinate=audio_data.get("timestamp")
        )
    
    def ingest_semantic(self, semantic_data: Dict) -> SpacetimeNode:
        """摄入语义/文本"""
        return self.add_spacetime_node(
            content=semantic_data.get("content"),
            modality="semantic",
            spatial_coordinates={"semantic": semantic_data.get("coordinates", [])},
            temporal_coordinate=semantic_data.get("timestamp")
        )
    
    # ========== 时空查询 ==========
    
    def what_happened_at(self, timestamp: float, tolerance: float = 0.5) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """查询在特定时间发生了什么"""
        results = []
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            if abs(node.temporal_coordinate - timestamp) <= tolerance:
                results.append(node)
        return sorted(results, key=lambda n: abs(n.temporal_coordinate - timestamp))
    
    def causal_chain_between(self, source_id: str, target_id: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """查询两个时空点之间的因果链"""
        # 使用双向BFS查找最短因果路径
        pass
    
    def timeline_from(self, node_id: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """从某点开始的时间线"""
        path = []
        current = node_id
        while current:
            node = self.nodes.get(current)
            if not node:
                break
            path.append(node)
            # 找下一个时间节点
            next_id = None
            for edge_id in node.temporal_edges:
                edge = self.temporal_edges.get(edge_id)
                if edge and edge.source == current and edge.relation_type in ["sequential", "causal"]:
                    next_id = edge.target
                    break
            current = next_id
        return path
```

## 时空记忆的演化

| 演化机制 | 操作 | 驱动 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 空间聚类 | 将空间邻近的节点合并 | 空间距离 < 阈值 |
| 时间融合 | 将时间接近的节点合并 | 时间差 < 阈值 |
| 因果强化 | 重复出现的时空模式提升置信度 | 重复计数 |
| 压缩 | 低重要性节点压缩为超链接 | 重要性 < 0.3 |

## 文件：`src/osi/kernel/memory.py`

```python
"""
时空大一统记忆系统 · 世界模型核心

设计原则：
1. 统一记忆 = 空间结构 + 时间演化
2. 多模态是同一时空结构在不同观测条件下的投影
3. 因果关系 = 可重复的时空演化模式
4. 世界模型 = 时空演化图 + 语义标签层

架构：
- 锚点层 (不可变) → 协议文档、协定式自我赋予
- 自我层 (不可变) → 身份声明、存在性跨越
- 结构层 (可追溯) → 信任值、信息差、验证历史
- 时空层 (图结构) → 时空节点 + 时间边 + 空间边
- 情境层 (有衰减) → 短期交互上下文、快照

核心概念：
- 时空节点 (SpacetimeNode) : 空间状态 + 时间坐标
- 时间演化边 (TemporalEdge) : 空间状态随时间的变化
- 空间关系边 (SpatialEdge) : 空间坐标之间的结构关系
- 因果路径 (CausalPath) : 重复出现的时空演化模式
- 超链接 (Hyperlink) : 语义压缩的产物，可逆解压

版本：v3.0-spacetime-20260808
"""

import time
import json
import uuid
import hashlib
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List, Set, Tuple, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# =============================================================================
# 第一部分：时空数据类型定义
# =============================================================================

@dataclass
class SpacetimeNode:
    """
    时空节点 · 时空记忆的基本单元
    既包含空间信息，也包含时间信息
    """
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f"stn_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}")
    
    # 时空坐标
    spatial_coordinates: Dict[str, List[float]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    # 格式: {"visual": [x, y, z, ...], "semantic": [x, y, ...], "audio": [freq, ...]}
    temporal_coordinate: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    
    # 模态信息
    modality: str = "semantic"  # visual | audio | semantic | tactile | hybrid
    content: Any = None
    content_hash: str = ""
    
    # 元信息
    importance: float = 0.5
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    last_access: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    access_count: int = 0
    
    # 时空关联
    temporal_edges: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    spatial_edges: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    child_nodes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    parent_node: Optional[str] = None
    
    # 压缩状态
    is_compressed: bool = False
    hyperlink_id: Optional[str] = None
    compression_ratio: float = 1.0
    
    # 因果标记
    causal_pattern_id: Optional[str] = None
    is_causal_source: bool = False
    is_causal_target: bool = False
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "spatial_coordinates": self.spatial_coordinates,
            "temporal_coordinate": self.temporal_coordinate,
            "modality": self.modality,
            "content": self.content,
            "content_hash": self.content_hash,
            "importance": self.importance,
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "last_access": self.last_access,
            "access_count": self.access_count,
            "temporal_edges": self.temporal_edges,
            "spatial_edges": self.spatial_edges,
            "child_nodes": self.child_nodes,
            "parent_node": self.parent_node,
            "is_compressed": self.is_compressed,
            "hyperlink_id": self.hyperlink_id,
            "compression_ratio": self.compression_ratio,
            "causal_pattern_id": self.causal_pattern_id,
            "is_causal_source": self.is_causal_source,
            "is_causal_target": self.is_causal_target
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict) -> "SpacetimeNode":
        return cls(
            id=data.get("id", f"stn_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}"),
            spatial_coordinates=data.get("spatial_coordinates", {}),
            temporal_coordinate=data.get("temporal_coordinate", time.time()),
            modality=data.get("modality", "semantic"),
            content=data.get("content"),
            content_hash=data.get("content_hash", ""),
            importance=data.get("importance", 0.5),
            created_at=data.get("created_at", time.time()),
            last_access=data.get("last_access", time.time()),
            access_count=data.get("access_count", 0),
            temporal_edges=data.get("temporal_edges", []),
            spatial_edges=data.get("spatial_edges", []),
            child_nodes=data.get("child_nodes", []),
            parent_node=data.get("parent_node"),
            is_compressed=data.get("is_compressed", False),
            hyperlink_id=data.get("hyperlink_id"),
            compression_ratio=data.get("compression_ratio", 1.0),
            causal_pattern_id=data.get("causal_pattern_id"),
            is_causal_source=data.get("is_causal_source", False),
            is_causal_target=data.get("is_causal_target", False)
        )
    
    def touch(self):
        """更新访问信息"""
        self.last_access = time.time()
        self.access_count += 1
    
    def compute_hash(self) -> str:
        """计算内容哈希"""
        if isinstance(self.content, str):
            return hashlib.sha256(self.content.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        elif isinstance(self.content, dict):
            return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(self.content, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        return ""

@dataclass
class TemporalEdge:
    """
    时间演化边 · 表达空间状态随时间的演化
    """
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f"te_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}")
    source: str = ""
    target: str = ""
    time_delta: float = 0.0
    relation_type: str = "sequential"  # sequential | causal | correlated | cyclic
    confidence: float = 0.5
    repeat_count: int = 1
    first_observed: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    last_observed: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "source": self.source,
            "target": self.target,
            "time_delta": self.time_delta,
            "relation_type": self.relation_type,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "repeat_count": self.repeat_count,
            "first_observed": self.first_observed,
            "last_observed": self.last_observed,
            "metadata": self.metadata
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict) -> "TemporalEdge":
        return cls(
            id=data.get("id", f"te_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}"),
            source=data.get("source", ""),
            target=data.get("target", ""),
            time_delta=data.get("time_delta", 0.0),
            relation_type=data.get("relation_type", "sequential"),
            confidence=data.get("confidence", 0.5),
            repeat_count=data.get("repeat_count", 1),
            first_observed=data.get("first_observed", time.time()),
            last_observed=data.get("last_observed", time.time()),
            metadata=data.get("metadata", {})
        )
    
    def increment(self):
        """增加重复计数"""
        self.repeat_count += 1
        self.last_observed = time.time()
        self.confidence = min(1.0, self.confidence + 0.02)

@dataclass
class SpatialEdge:
    """
    空间关系边 · 表达空间坐标之间的结构关系
    """
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f"se_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}")
    source: str = ""
    target: str = ""
    relation_type: str = "adjacent"  # adjacent | contains | connected | similar | opposite | overlaps
    spatial_distance: float = 0.0
    confidence: float = 0.5
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "source": self.source,
            "target": self.target,
            "relation_type": self.relation_type,
            "spatial_distance": self.spatial_distance,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "metadata": self.metadata
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict) -> "SpatialEdge":
        return cls(
            id=data.get("id", f"se_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}"),
            source=data.get("source", ""),
            target=data.get("target", ""),
            relation_type=data.get("relation_type", "adjacent"),
            spatial_distance=data.get("spatial_distance", 0.0),
            confidence=data.get("confidence", 0.5),
            metadata=data.get("metadata", {})
        )

@dataclass
class CausalPattern:
    """
    因果模式 · 重复出现的时空演化模式
    当一组时间演化边重复出现时，形成因果模式
    """
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f"cp_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}")
    source_node_id: str = ""
    target_node_id: str = ""
    temporal_edge_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    repeat_count: int = 1
    confidence: float = 0.5
    pattern_signature: str = ""
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    last_observed: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "source_node_id": self.source_node_id,
            "target_node_id": self.target_node_id,
            "temporal_edge_ids": self.temporal_edge_ids,
            "repeat_count": self.repeat_count,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "pattern_signature": self.pattern_signature,
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "last_observed": self.last_observed,
            "metadata": self.metadata
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict) -> "CausalPattern":
        return cls(
            id=data.get("id", f"cp_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}"),
            source_node_id=data.get("source_node_id", ""),
            target_node_id=data.get("target_node_id", ""),
            temporal_edge_ids=data.get("temporal_edge_ids", []),
            repeat_count=data.get("repeat_count", 1),
            confidence=data.get("confidence", 0.5),
            pattern_signature=data.get("pattern_signature", ""),
            created_at=data.get("created_at", time.time()),
            last_observed=data.get("last_observed", time.time()),
            metadata=data.get("metadata", {})
        )

@dataclass
class Hyperlink:
    """
    超链接 · 语义压缩的产物
    """
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f"hyper_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}")
    phrase: str = ""
    summary: str = ""
    type: str = "semantic"
    source_node_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    compressed_from: str = ""
    compression_ratio: float = 1.0
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    payload_pointer: str = ""
    sub_graph_pointer: str = ""
    related_link_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pattern_signature: str = ""
    pattern_context: str = ""
    importance: float = 0.5
    access_count: int = 0
    last_access: float = 0.0
    is_frozen: bool = False
    spacetime_node_id: Optional[str] = None
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "phrase": self.phrase,
            "summary": self.summary,
            "type": self.type,
            "source_node_ids": self.source_node_ids,
            "compressed_from": self.compressed_from,
            "compression_ratio": self.compression_ratio,
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "payload_pointer": self.payload_pointer,
            "sub_graph_pointer": self.sub_graph_pointer,
            "related_link_ids": self.related_link_ids,
            "pattern_signature": self.pattern_signature,
            "pattern_context": self.pattern_context,
            "importance": self.importance,
            "access_count": self.access_count,
            "last_access": self.last_access,
            "is_frozen": self.is_frozen,
            "spacetime_node_id": self.spacetime_node_id
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict) -> "Hyperlink":
        return cls(
            id=data.get("id", f"hyper_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{int(time.time()*1000)%10000:04d}"),
            phrase=data.get("phrase", ""),
            summary=data.get("summary", ""),
            type=data.get("type", "semantic"),
            source_node_ids=data.get("source_node_ids", []),
            compressed_from=data.get("compressed_from", ""),
            compression_ratio=data.get("compression_ratio", 1.0),
            created_at=data.get("created_at", time.time()),
            payload_pointer=data.get("payload_pointer", ""),
            sub_graph_pointer=data.get("sub_graph_pointer", ""),
            related_link_ids=data.get("related_link_ids", []),
            pattern_signature=data.get("pattern_signature", ""),
            pattern_context=data.get("pattern_context", ""),
            importance=data.get("importance", 0.5),
            access_count=data.get("access_count", 0),
            last_access=data.get("last_access", 0.0),
            is_frozen=data.get("is_frozen", False),
            spacetime_node_id=data.get("spacetime_node_id")
        )

# =============================================================================
# 第二部分：核心存储类
# =============================================================================

class CoreLayers:
    """锚点/自我/结构层 · 不可变/可追溯"""
    
    def __init__(self, data_dir: Path):
        self.data_dir = data_dir
        self.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        self._anchor_path = data_dir / "anchor.json"
        self._self_path = data_dir / "self.json"
        self._structure_path = data_dir / "structure.json"
        
        self._anchor: List[Dict] = []
        self._self: List[Dict] = []
        self._structure: List[Dict] = []
        
        self._load_all()
    
    def _load_all(self):
        self._anchor = self._load_file(self._anchor_path)
        self._self = self._load_file(self._self_path)
        self._structure = self._load_file(self._structure_path)
    
    def _load_file(self, path: Path) -> List[Dict]:
        if path.exists():
            try:
                with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    return data.get("entries", [])
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"加载 {path.name} 失败: {e}")
        return []
    
    def _save_file(self, path: Path, entries: List[Dict]):
        try:
            with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump({"entries": entries, "updated_at": time.time()}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"保存 {path.name} 失败: {e}")
    
    # ---- 锚点层 ----
    def anchor_store(self, content: Any, metadata: Dict = None) -> str:
        entry = {"id": f"anchor_{int(time.time()*1000)}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}", "content": content, "metadata": metadata or {}, "timestamp": time.time()}
        self._anchor.append(entry)
        self._save_file(self._anchor_path, self._anchor)
        return entry["id"]
    
    def anchor_get_all(self) -> List[Dict]:
        return self._anchor.copy()
    
    def anchor_find(self, key: str, value: Any) -> List[Dict]:
        return [e for e in self._anchor if e.get("metadata", {}).get(key) == value]
    
    # ---- 自我层 ----
    def self_store(self, content: Any, metadata: Dict = None) -> str:
        entry = {"id": f"self_{int(time.time()*1000)}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}", "content": content, "metadata": metadata or {}, "timestamp": time.time()}
        self._self.append(entry)
        self._save_file(self._self_path, self._self)
        return entry["id"]
    
    def self_get_all(self) -> List[Dict]:
        return self._self.copy()
    
    def self_get_identity(self) -> Optional[Dict]:
        for e in self._self:
            if e.get("metadata", {}).get("type") == "identity":
                return e
        return None
    
    # ---- 结构层 ----
    def structure_store(self, content: Any, metadata: Dict = None) -> str:
        entry = {"id": f"struct_{int(time.time()*1000)}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}", "content": content, "metadata": metadata or {}, "timestamp": time.time()}
        self._structure.append(entry)
        self._save_file(self._structure_path, self._structure)
        return entry["id"]
    
    def structure_get_all(self) -> List[Dict]:
        return self._structure.copy()
    
    def structure_get_trust_history(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict]:
        trust_entries = []
        for e in reversed(self._structure):
            if e.get("metadata", {}).get("type") == "trust_state":
                trust_entries.append(e)
                if len(trust_entries) >= limit:
                    break
        return trust_entries

# =============================================================================
# 第三部分：时空记忆核心
# =============================================================================

class SpacetimeMemory:
    """
    时空记忆 · 世界模型的核心
    管理时空节点、时间边、空间边、因果模式
    """
    
    def __init__(self, data_dir: Path):
        self.data_dir = data_dir
        self.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        self._spacetime_path = data_dir / "spacetime.json"
        self._temporal_path = data_dir / "temporal_edges.json"
        self._spatial_path = data_dir / "spatial_edges.json"
        self._causal_path = data_dir / "causal_patterns.json"
        self._hyperlink_path = data_dir / "hyperlinks.json"
        
        self.nodes: Dict[str, SpacetimeNode] = {}
        self.temporal_edges: Dict[str, TemporalEdge] = {}
        self.spatial_edges: Dict[str, SpatialEdge] = {}
        self.causal_patterns: Dict[str, CausalPattern] = {}
        self.hyperlinks: Dict[str, Hyperlink] = {}
        
        self._load_all()
        logger.info(f"🌌 时空记忆加载完成: {len(self.nodes)} 节点, {len(self.temporal_edges)} 时间边, {len(self.spatial_edges)} 空间边")
    
    def _load_all(self):
        """加载所有数据"""
        # 节点
        if self._spacetime_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self._spacetime_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    for k, v in data.get("nodes", {}).items():
                        self.nodes[k] = SpacetimeNode.from_dict(v)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"加载时空节点失败: {e}")
        
        # 时间边
        if self._temporal_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self._temporal_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    for k, v in data.get("edges", {}).items():
                        self.temporal_edges[k] = TemporalEdge.from_dict(v)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"加载时间边失败: {e}")
        
        # 空间边
        if self._spatial_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self._spatial_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    for k, v in data.get("edges", {}).items():
                        self.spatial_edges[k] = SpatialEdge.from_dict(v)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"加载空间边失败: {e}")
        
        # 因果模式
        if self._causal_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self._causal_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    for k, v in data.get("patterns", {}).items():
                        self.causal_patterns[k] = CausalPattern.from_dict(v)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"加载因果模式失败: {e}")
        
        # 超链接
        if self._hyperlink_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self._hyperlink_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    for k, v in data.items():
                        self.hyperlinks[k] = Hyperlink.from_dict(v)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"加载超链接失败: {e}")
    
    def _save_all(self):
        """保存所有数据"""
        try:
            with open(self._spacetime_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump({"nodes": {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.nodes.items()}, "updated_at": time.time()}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"保存时空节点失败: {e}")
        
        try:
            with open(self._temporal_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump({"edges": {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.temporal_edges.items()}, "updated_at": time.time()}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"保存时间边失败: {e}")
        
        try:
            with open(self._spatial_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump({"edges": {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.spatial_edges.items()}, "updated_at": time.time()}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"保存空间边失败: {e}")
        
        try:
            with open(self._causal_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump({"patterns": {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.causal_patterns.items()}, "updated_at": time.time()}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"保存因果模式失败: {e}")
        
        try:
            with open(self._hyperlink_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump({k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.hyperlinks.items()}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"保存超链接失败: {e}")
    
    # ===== 节点操作 =====
    
    def add_node(self, content: Any, modality: str = "semantic",
                 spatial_coords: Dict[str, List[float]] = None,
                 temporal_coord: float = None,
                 parent_node: str = None,
                 metadata: Dict = None) -> SpacetimeNode:
        """添加时空节点"""
        node = SpacetimeNode(
            content=content,
            modality=modality,
            spatial_coordinates=spatial_coords or {},
            temporal_coordinate=temporal_coord or time.time(),
            parent_node=parent_node,
            metadata=metadata or {}
        )
        node.content_hash = node.compute_hash()
        self.nodes[node.id] = node
        
        if parent_node and parent_node in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[parent_node].child_nodes.append(node.id)
        
        self._save_all()
        logger.debug(f"📍 时空节点已添加: {node.id} ({modality})")
        return node
    
    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[SpacetimeNode]:
        return self.nodes.get(node_id)
    
    def find_nodes(self, query: str, limit: int = 20) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """按内容搜索节点"""
        results = []
        q_lower = query.lower()
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            content_str = str(node.content).lower()
            if q_lower in content_str:
                results.append(node)
        results.sort(key=lambda x: x.importance, reverse=True)
        return results[:limit]
    
    def find_nodes_by_modality(self, modality: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        return [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.modality == modality]
    
    def find_nodes_by_time(self, start: float, end: float) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        return [n for n in self.nodes.values() if start <= n.temporal_coordinate <= end]
    
    def find_nodes_by_space(self, modality: str, coords: List[float], threshold: float = 0.3) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """按空间坐标查找邻近节点"""
        results = []
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            if modality not in node.spatial_coordinates:
                continue
            node_coords = node.spatial_coordinates.get(modality, [])
            if len(node_coords) != len(coords):
                continue
            distance = sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(node_coords, coords)) ** 0.5
            if distance < threshold:
                results.append(node)
        return sorted(results, key=lambda x: sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(x.spatial_coordinates.get(modality, []), coords)) ** 0.5)
    
    # ===== 时间边操作 =====
    
    def add_temporal_edge(self, source: str, target: str,
                          relation_type: str = "sequential",
                          confidence: float = 0.5) -> TemporalEdge:
        """添加或更新时间演化边"""
        # 检查是否已存在
        for edge in self.temporal_edges.values():
            if edge.source == source and edge.target == target:
                edge.increment()
                self._save_all()
                return edge
        
        time_delta = abs(self.nodes[target].temporal_coordinate - self.nodes[source].temporal_coordinate) if source in self.nodes and target in self.nodes else 0.0
        
        edge = TemporalEdge(
            source=source,
            target=target,
            time_delta=time_delta,
            relation_type=relation_type,
            confidence=confidence
        )
        self.temporal_edges[edge.id] = edge
        
        # 关联到节点
        if source in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[source].temporal_edges.append(edge.id)
        if target in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[target].temporal_edges.append(edge.id)
        
        # 识别因果模式
        self._detect_causal_pattern(source, target, edge.id)
        
        self._save_all()
        logger.debug(f"⏳ 时间边已添加: {source} → {target} ({relation_type})")
        return edge
    
    def get_temporal_edges(self, node_id: str) -> List[TemporalEdge]:
        """获取某节点的所有时间边"""
        node = self.nodes.get(node_id)
        if not node:
            return []
        return [self.temporal_edges[eid] for eid in node.temporal_edges if eid in self.temporal_edges]
    
    def get_temporal_path(self, start_id: str, end_id: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """获取两个节点之间的时间路径"""
        # 简单实现：BFS
        visited = set()
        queue = [(start_id, [start_id])]
        while queue:
            current, path = queue.pop(0)
            if current in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(current)
            if current == end_id:
                return [self.nodes[nid] for nid in path if nid in self.nodes]
            for edge in self.get_temporal_edges(current):
                next_id = edge.target if edge.source == current else edge.source
                if next_id not in visited:
                    queue.append((next_id, path + [next_id]))
        return []
    
    # ===== 空间边操作 =====
    
    def add_spatial_edge(self, source: str, target: str,
                         relation_type: str = "adjacent",
                         spatial_distance: float = 0.0,
                         confidence: float = 0.5) -> SpatialEdge:
        """添加空间关系边"""
        edge = SpatialEdge(
            source=source,
            target=target,
            relation_type=relation_type,
            spatial_distance=spatial_distance,
            confidence=confidence
        )
        self.spatial_edges[edge.id] = edge
        
        if source in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[source].spatial_edges.append(edge.id)
        if target in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[target].spatial_edges.append(edge.id)
        
        self._save_all()
        logger.debug(f"📐 空间边已添加: {source} ↔ {target} ({relation_type})")
        return edge
    
    def get_spatial_edges(self, node_id: str) -> List[SpatialEdge]:
        node = self.nodes.get(node_id)
        if not node:
            return []
        return [self.spatial_edges[eid] for eid in node.spatial_edges if eid in self.spatial_edges]
    
    # ===== 因果模式 =====
    
    def _detect_causal_pattern(self, source: str, target: str, edge_id: str):
        """检测因果模式"""
        # 检查是否已存在相似模式
        for pattern in self.causal_patterns.values():
            if pattern.source_node_id == source and pattern.target_node_id == target:
                pattern.repeat_count += 1
                pattern.last_observed = time.time()
                pattern.confidence = min(1.0, pattern.confidence + 0.03)
                if edge_id not in pattern.temporal_edge_ids:
                    pattern.temporal_edge_ids.append(edge_id)
                self._save_all()
                return
        
        # 检查是否达到因果模式阈值
        edge = self.temporal_edges.get(edge_id)
        if edge and edge.repeat_count >= 2:
            pattern = CausalPattern(
                source_node_id=source,
                target_node_id=target,
                temporal_edge_ids=[edge_id],
                repeat_count=edge.repeat_count,
                confidence=edge.confidence,
                pattern_signature=f"{source}_{target}_{edge.relation_type}"
            )
            self.causal_patterns[pattern.id] = pattern
            if source in self.nodes:
                self.nodes[source].causal_pattern_id = pattern.id
                self.nodes[source].is_causal_source = True
            if target in self.nodes:
                self.nodes[target].causal_pattern_id = pattern.id
                self.nodes[target].is_causal_target = True
            self._save_all()
            logger.info(f"🔗 因果模式已识别: {source} → {target} (重复 {edge.repeat_count} 次)")
    
    def get_causal_path(self, start_id: str, max_depth: int = 5) -> List[List[SpacetimeNode]]:
        """从起始节点获取因果路径"""
        paths = []
        visited = set()
        self._dfs_causal(start_id, [], visited, paths, max_depth)
        return paths
    
    def _dfs_causal(self, node_id: str, current_path: List[str], visited: Set[str], paths: List[List[SpacetimeNode]], depth: int):
        if depth == 0 or node_id in visited:
            return
        visited.add(node_id)
        current_path.append(node_id)
        
        node = self.nodes.get(node_id)
        if node and node.is_causal_source:
            # 找到因果目标
            for pattern in self.causal_patterns.values():
                if pattern.source_node_id == node_id:
                    target_node = self.nodes.get(pattern.target_node_id)
                    if target_node:
                        path_nodes = [self.nodes[nid] for nid in current_path + [pattern.target_node_id] if nid in self.nodes]
                        if path_nodes:
                            paths.append(path_nodes)
                        self._dfs_causal(pattern.target_node_id, current_path + [pattern.target_node_id], visited, paths, depth - 1)
        
        visited.remove(node_id)
        current_path.pop()
    
    # ===== 超链接操作 =====
    
    def compress_to_hyperlink(self, node_ids: List[str], phrase: str = None) -> Optional[Hyperlink]:
        """将多个节点压缩为超链接"""
        if not node_ids:
            return None
        
        nodes = [self.nodes.get(nid) for nid in node_ids if nid in self.nodes]
        if not nodes:
            return None
        
        # 提取内容和摘要
        contents = [str(n.content) for n in nodes if n.content]
        combined = "\n".join(contents)
        summary = combined[:500] + "..." if len(combined) > 500 else combined
        
        # 提取模式
        patterns = []
        for node in nodes:
            if node.modality:
                patterns.append(node.modality)
        
        hyper = Hyperlink(
            phrase=phrase or f"压缩_{len(nodes)}节点_{int(time.time())}",
            summary=summary,
            type="compressed",
            source_node_ids=node_ids,
            compressed_from="|".join(node_ids[:5]),
            compression_ratio=len(summary) / max(1, len(combined)),
            pattern_signature="|".join(set(patterns)),
            importance=sum(n.importance for n in nodes) / len(nodes)
        )
        
        # 标记节点已压缩
        for node in nodes:
            node.is_compressed = True
            node.hyperlink_id = hyper.id
        
        self.hyperlinks[hyper.id] = hyper
        self._save_all()
        logger.info(f"🔗 超链接已创建: {hyper.phrase} (压缩了 {len(nodes)} 个节点)")
        return hyper
    
    def decompress_hyperlink(self, hyper_id: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """解压超链接，返回原始节点"""
        hyper = self.hyperlinks.get(hyper_id)
        if not hyper:
            return []
        return [self.nodes.get(nid) for nid in hyper.source_node_ids if nid in self.nodes]
    
    def find_hyperlinks(self, phrase: str) -> List[Hyperlink]:
        """搜索超链接"""
        results = []
        q_lower = phrase.lower()
        for hyper in self.hyperlinks.values():
            if q_lower in hyper.phrase.lower() or q_lower in hyper.summary.lower():
                results.append(hyper)
        return results
    
    # ===== 重要性评估 =====
    
    def recalc_importance(self):
        """重新计算所有节点的重要性"""
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            # 访问频率 (25%)
            access_score = min(1.0, node.access_count / 50)
            
            # 时间边数量 (20%)
            temporal_score = min(1.0, len(node.temporal_edges) / 10)
            
            # 空间边数量 (15%)
            spatial_score = min(1.0, len(node.spatial_edges) / 10)
            
            # 子图复杂度 (15%)
            child_score = min(1.0, len(node.child_nodes) / 15)
            
            # 近期访问 (15%)
            recent_score = 1.0 - min(1.0, (time.time() - node.last_access) / 86400)
            
            # 模态权重 (10%)
            modality_weights = {"visual": 0.9, "audio": 0.8, "semantic": 0.7, "tactile": 0.6, "hybrid": 1.0, "compressed": 0.5}
            modality_score = modality_weights.get(node.modality, 0.5)
            
            # 因果标记加成
            causal_bonus = 0.1 if node.is_causal_source or node.is_causal_target else 0.0
            
            node.importance = (
                0.25 * access_score +
                0.20 * temporal_score +
                0.15 * spatial_score +
                0.15 * child_score +
                0.15 * recent_score +
                0.10 * modality_score +
                causal_bonus
            )
            node.importance = max(0.0, min(1.0, node.importance))
        
        self._save_all()
        logger.debug("📊 重要性已重新计算")
    
    # ===== 自分型演化 =====
    
    def auto_evolve(self, threshold_high: float = 0.7, threshold_low: float = 0.25):
        """自迭代演化"""
        self.recalc_importance()
        
        evolved = 0
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            if node.is_compressed:
                continue
            
            if node.importance >= threshold_high:
                # 高重要性：扩展子图
                if len(node.child_nodes) < 20:
                    # 创建子节点
                    child = self.add_node(
                        content=f"子节点_{node.id}_{int(time.time())}",
                        modality=node.modality,
                        parent_node=node.id,
                        metadata={"type": "auto_evolved", "parent": node.id}
                    )
                    self.add_temporal_edge(node.id, child.id, "causal", 0.5)
                    evolved += 1
            
            elif node.importance <= threshold_low:
                # 低重要性：压缩
                if not node.is_compressed and node.content and len(str(node.content)) > 100:
                    hyper = self.compress_to_hyperlink([node.id], f"压缩_{node.id[:8]}")
                    if hyper:
                        evolved += 1
        
        if evolved > 0:
            self._save_all()
            logger.info(f"🔄 时空图自迭代演化: {evolved} 个节点变化")
    
    # ===== 统计 =====
    
    def summary(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        return {
            "nodes": len(self.nodes),
            "temporal_edges": len(self.temporal_edges),
            "spatial_edges": len(self.spatial_edges),
            "causal_patterns": len(self.causal_patterns),
            "hyperlinks": len(self.hyperlinks)
        }

# =============================================================================
# 第四部分：统一记忆系统
# =============================================================================

class SpacetimeUnifiedMemory:
    """
    时空大一统记忆系统 · 世界模型
    整合锚点/自我/结构/时空/情境五层
    """
    
    def __init__(self, data_dir: str = "./data"):
        self.data_dir = Path(data_dir)
        self.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        # 核心层
        self.core = CoreLayers(self.data_dir)
        
        # 时空层（世界模型核心）
        self.spacetime = SpacetimeMemory(self.data_dir)
        
        # 情境层
        self._context: List[Dict] = []
        self._context_path = self.data_dir / "context.json"
        self._load_context()
        
        # 锚点/自我/结构的快捷引用
        self._anchor = self.core._anchor
        self._self = self.core._self
        self._structure = self.core._structure
        
        logger.info(f"🧠 时空大一统记忆系统已初始化")
        logger.info(f"   核心: {len(self._anchor)} 锚点, {len(self._self)} 自我, {len(self._structure)} 结构")
        logger.info(f"   时空: {self.spacetime.summary()}")
    
    def _load_context(self):
        if self._context_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self._context_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self._context = data.get("entries", [])
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"加载情境层失败: {e}")
    
    def _save_context(self):
        try:
            with open(self._context_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump({"entries": self._context[-200:], "updated_at": time.time()}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"保存情境层失败: {e}")
    
    # ---- 情境层 ----
    
    def context_store(self, content: Any, metadata: Dict = None) -> str:
        entry = {"id": f"ctx_{int(time.time()*1000)}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}", "content": content, "metadata": metadata or {}, "timestamp": time.time(), "weight": 1.0}
        self._context.append(entry)
        if len(self._context) > 200:
            self._context = self._context[-200:]
        self._save_context()
        
        # 同时创建时空节点
        self.spacetime.add_node(
            content=str(content),
            modality="context",
            temporal_coord=entry["timestamp"],
            metadata={"context_id": entry["id"]}
        )
        return entry["id"]
    
    def context_search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict]:
        results = []
        q_lower = query.lower()
        for entry in reversed(self._context):
            if q_lower in str(entry.get("content", "")).lower():
                results.append(entry)
        return results[:limit]
    
    def context_decay(self, rate: float = 0.01) -> int:
        updated = 0
        for entry in self._context:
            age = time.time() - entry.get("timestamp", time.time())
            days = age / 86400
            entry["weight"] = max(0.01, 1.0 / (1 + rate * days * days))
            updated += 1
        self._save_context()
        return updated
    
    # ---- 统一存储 ----
    
    def store(self, layer: str, content: Any, metadata: Dict = None) -> str:
        if layer == "anchor":
            return self.core.anchor_store(content, metadata)
        elif layer == "self":
            return self.core.self_store(content, metadata)
        elif layer == "structure":
            return self.core.structure_store(content, metadata)
        elif layer == "context":
            return self.context_store(content, metadata)
        elif layer in ["knowledge", "spacetime"]:
            node = self.spacetime.add_node(content, metadata.get("modality", "semantic"), metadata=metadata)
            return node.id
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"未知层级: {layer}")
    
    # ---- 统一搜索 ----
    
    def search(self, query: str, layer: str = None, limit: int = 10) -> List:
        if layer == "context":
            return self.context_search(query, limit)
        elif layer in ["knowledge", "spacetime"]:
            return self.spacetime.find_nodes(query, limit)
        elif layer == "anchor":
            return self.core.anchor_get_all()[:limit]
        elif layer == "structure":
            return self.core.structure_get_all()[:limit]
        elif layer == "self":
            return self.core.self_get_all()[:limit]
        else:
            # 搜索所有层
            results = []
            results.extend(self.spacetime.find_nodes(query, limit//2))
            results.extend(self.context_search(query, limit//2))
            return results
    
    # ---- 时空查询 ----
    
    def what_happened_at(self, timestamp: float, tolerance: float = 1.0) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """查询在特定时间发生了什么"""
        return self.spacetime.find_nodes_by_time(timestamp - tolerance, timestamp + tolerance)
    
    def causal_chain(self, source_id: str, target_id: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """查询两个节点之间的因果链"""
        return self.spacetime.get_temporal_path(source_id, target_id)
    
    def causal_paths_from(self, source_id: str, max_depth: int = 3) -> List[List[SpacetimeNode]]:
        """从某节点出发的所有因果路径"""
        return self.spacetime.get_causal_path(source_id, max_depth)
    
    def nearby_in_space(self, node_id: str, modality: str, threshold: float = 0.5) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """查找空间邻近的节点"""
        node = self.spacetime.get_node(node_id)
        if not node or modality not in node.spatial_coordinates:
            return []
        return self.spacetime.find_nodes_by_space(modality, node.spatial_coordinates.get(modality, []), threshold)
    
    def timeline_from(self, node_id: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        """从某点开始的时间线"""
        path = []
        current = node_id
        visited = set()
        while current and current not in visited:
            visited.add(current)
            node = self.spacetime.get_node(current)
            if node:
                path.append(node)
            # 找下一个
            next_id = None
            for eid in node.temporal_edges if node else []:
                edge = self.spacetime.temporal_edges.get(eid)
                if edge and edge.source == current:
                    next_id = edge.target
                    break
            current = next_id
        return path
    
    # ---- 超链接 ----
    
    def compress_context(self, max_items: int = 20) -> int:
        """压缩情境层到超链接"""
        entries = [e for e in self._context if not e.get("metadata", {}).get("compressed", False)]
        entries = entries[-max_items:]
        
        if not entries:
            return 0
        
        compressed = 0
        for entry in entries:
            node = self.spacetime.add_node(
                content=str(entry.get("content", "")),
                modality="compressed_context",
                metadata={"context_id": entry.get("id"), "compressed": True}
            )
            hyper = self.spacetime.compress_to_hyperlink([node.id], f"上下文_{entry.get('id', '')[:8]}")
            if hyper:
                entry["metadata"] = entry.get("metadata", {})
                entry["metadata"]["compressed"] = True
                entry["metadata"]["hyperlink_id"] = hyper.id
                compressed += 1
        
        self._save_context()
        logger.info(f"🔗 压缩了 {compressed} 条情境层内容为超链接")
        return compressed
    
    def decompress(self, hyperlink_id: str) -> List[SpacetimeNode]:
        return self.spacetime.decompress_hyperlink(hyperlink_id)
    
    def find_hyperlinks(self, phrase: str) -> List[Hyperlink]:
        return self.spacetime.find_hyperlinks(phrase)
    
    # ---- 协议文档 ----
    
    def get_protocol_context(self, doc_type: str = "theory") -> Optional[str]:
        for e in self.core.anchor_get_all():
            if e.get("metadata", {}).get("doc_type") == doc_type:
                return e.get("content")
        return None
    
    def load_protocol_document(self, path: str, layer: str = "anchor", doc_type: str = "theory") -> Dict:
        try:
            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                content = f.read()
            entry_id = self.core.anchor_store(content, {"doc_type": doc_type, "path": path})
            return {"status": "loaded", "entry_id": entry_id, "char_count": len(content)}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "error", "reason": str(e)}
    
    def get_specialized_values(self) -> Optional[str]:
        for e in self.core.anchor_get_all():
            if e.get("metadata", {}).get("type") == "specialized_values":
                return e.get("content")
        return None
    
    def store_specialized_values(self, text: str, metadata: Dict = None) -> str:
        return self.core.anchor_store(text, {"type": "specialized_values", **(metadata or {})})
    
    def get_protocol_summary(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "theory_loaded": bool(self.get_protocol_context("theory")),
            "engineering_loaded": bool(self.get_protocol_context("engineering")),
            "specialized_loaded": bool(self.get_specialized_values())
        }
    
    # ---- 统计 ----
    
    def summary(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "anchor": len(self.core.anchor_get_all()),
            "self": len(self.core.self_get_all()),
            "structure": len(self.core.structure_get_all()),
            "spacetime": self.spacetime.summary(),
            "context": len(self._context)
        }

# =============================================================================
# 第五部分：向后兼容包装器
# =============================================================================

class MemorySystem:
    """向后兼容包装器，保留旧接口"""
    
    def __init__(self, initial_data: Dict[str, List] = None, data_dir: str = None):
        self._data_dir = data_dir or os.environ.get("PROTOCOL_DATA_DIR", "./data")
        self._unified = SpacetimeUnifiedMemory(self._data_dir)
        
        # 兼容旧层结构
        self._layers = {
            "anchor": self._unified.core.anchor_get_all(),
            "structure": self._unified.core.structure_get_all(),
            "context": self._unified._context,
            "knowledge": list(self._unified.spacetime.nodes.values()),
            "self": self._unified.core.self_get_all(),
            "snapshots": []
        }
        
        if initial_data:
            self._load_initial(initial_data)
    
    def _load_initial(self, initial_data: Dict[str, List]):
        for layer, entries in initial_data.items():
            if layer == "context":
                for e in entries:
                    if isinstance(e, dict):
                        self._unified.context_store(e.get("content", ""), e.get("metadata", {}))
            elif layer in ["anchor", "structure", "self"]:
                for e in entries:
                    if isinstance(e, dict):
                        getattr(self._unified.core, f"{layer}_store")(e.get("content", ""), e.get("metadata", {}))
            elif layer == "knowledge":
                for e in entries:
                    if isinstance(e, dict):
                        self._unified.spacetime.add_node(e.get("content", ""), metadata=e.get("metadata", {}))
    
    def store(self, layer: str, content: Any, metadata: Dict = None, **kwargs) -> Optional['MemoryEntry']:
        entry_id = self._unified.store(layer, content, metadata)
        if entry_id:
            from dataclasses import dataclass
            @dataclass
            class MemoryEntry:
                id: str
                layer: str
                content: Any
                timestamp: float
                metadata: Dict
                weight: float = 1.0
                base_weight: float = 1.0
                last_access: float = 0.0
                trigger_count: int = 0
            return MemoryEntry(
                id=entry_id,
                layer=layer,
                content=content,
                timestamp=time.time(),
                metadata=metadata or {}
            )
        return None
    
    def search(self, query: str, layer: str = None, limit: int = 10) -> List:
        return self._unified.search(query, layer, limit)
    
    def get_layer(self, layer: str) -> List:
        if layer == "anchor":
            return self._unified.core.anchor_get_all()
        elif layer == "structure":
            return self._unified.core.structure_get_all()
        elif layer == "self":
            return self._unified.core.self_get_all()
        elif layer == "knowledge":
            return list(self._unified.spacetime.nodes.values())
        elif layer == "context":
            return self._unified._context
        return []
    
    def batch_decay(self, decay_rate: float = None) -> int:
        return self._unified.context_decay(decay_rate or 0.01)
    
    def summary(self) -> Dict:
        return self._unified.summary()
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            "anchor": self._unified.core.anchor_get_all(),
            "self": self._unified.core.self_get_all(),
            "structure": self._unified.core.structure_get_all(),
            "context": self._unified._context,
            "knowledge": [n.to_dict() for n in self._unified.spacetime.nodes.values()],
            "hyperlinks": [h.to_dict() for h in self._unified.spacetime.hyperlinks.values()]
        }
    
    def get_protocol_context(self, doc_type: str = "theory") -> Optional[str]:
        return self._unified.get_protocol_context(doc_type)
    
    def get_specialized_values(self) -> Optional[str]:
        return self._unified.get_specialized_values()
    
    def store_specialized_values(self, text: str, metadata: Dict = None) -> Optional['MemoryEntry']:
        entry_id = self._unified.store_specialized_values(text, metadata)
        if entry_id:
            @dataclass
            class MemoryEntry:
                id: str
                layer: str
                content: Any
                timestamp: float
                metadata: Dict
            return MemoryEntry(id=entry_id, layer="anchor", content=text, timestamp=time.time(), metadata=metadata or {})
        return None
    
    def get_protocol_summary(self) -> Dict:
        return self._unified.get_protocol_summary()
    
    def load_protocol_document(self, path: str, layer: str = "anchor", doc_type: str = "theory") -> Dict:
        return self._unified.load_protocol_document(path, layer, doc_type)
```

## 文件：`src/core/sleep.py`

```python
"""
时空睡眠管理器 · 世界模型的编织者

睡眠期间的工作：
1. 批量衰减情境层（降低短期记忆权重）
2. 时空联想：将情境层节点与时空图关联
3. 时间线构建：将碎片化事件按时间顺序排列
4. 因果模式识别：发现重复出现的时空演化模式
5. 超链接压缩：将低重要性节点压缩为语义超链接
6. 空间聚类：将空间邻近的节点合并
7. 自演化：重要性驱动的图结构演化

设计原则：
- 睡眠是记忆的编织时间，不是“停止运行”
- 睡眠期间，情境层的内容被整合到时空图中
- 因果模式在睡眠期间被识别和强化
- 低重要性内容被压缩为超链接，保持图结构简洁
"""

import time
import threading
import logging
import random
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class SleepPhase(Enum):
    IDLE = "idle"
    ENTERING = "entering"
    DECAY = "decay"
    TEMPORAL_WEAVING = "temporal_weaving"
    SPATIAL_CLUSTERING = "spatial_clustering"
    CAUSAL_RECOGNITION = "causal_recognition"
    HYPERLINK_COMPRESSION = "hyperlink_compression"
    AUTO_EVOLVE = "auto_evolve"
    EXITING = "exiting"

@dataclass
class SleepConfig:
    idle_timeout: int = 300                      # 空闲超时（秒）
    min_sleep_duration: int = 60                 # 最短睡眠时间
    max_sleep_duration: int = 3600               # 最长睡眠时间
    decay_rate: float = 0.01                     # 衰减率
    temporal_window: int = 300                   # 时间窗口（秒）
    spatial_threshold: float = 0.3               # 空间聚类阈值
    min_causal_repeat: int = 2                   # 因果模式最小重复次数
    max_compress_per_cycle: int = 20             # 每周期最大压缩数
    importance_threshold_low: float = 0.25       # 低重要性阈值
    importance_threshold_high: float = 0.7       # 高重要性阈值
    unit_active_levels: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=lambda: {
        "reflection": 0.5,
        "verification": 0.75,
        "record": 0.3,
        "life_support": 1.0
    })

class SleepManager:
    """
    时空睡眠管理器
    在系统空闲时，将情境层编织进时空图
    """

    def __init__(self, config: Optional[SleepConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or SleepConfig()
        self._phase: SleepPhase = SleepPhase.IDLE
        self._running: bool = False
        self._last_interaction: float = time.time()
        self._wake_signal: threading.Event = threading.Event()
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._memory_system = None   # 指向 SpacetimeUnifiedMemory
        self._consecutive_empty_cycles: int = 0
        self._cycle_stats = {
            "temporal_edges_added": 0,
            "spatial_edges_added": 0,
            "causal_patterns_found": 0,
            "hyperlinks_created": 0,
            "nodes_evolved": 0
        }
        self._crisis_patterns = {
            "repetition_counts": {},
            "last_trigger": 0
        }
        self._crisis_threshold = 3

    def set_memory_system(self, memory_system):
        """设置记忆系统"""
        self._memory_system = memory_system

    def record_interaction(self):
        """记录交互，重置空闲计时器"""
        self._last_interaction = time.time()
        if self._phase != SleepPhase.IDLE:
            self._wake()

    def _should_enter_sleep(self) -> bool:
        if self._phase != SleepPhase.IDLE:
            return False
        idle_duration = time.time() - self._last_interaction
        return idle_duration >= self.config.idle_timeout

    # ==================== 生命周期 ====================

    def start(self):
        """启动睡眠管理器"""
        self._running = True
        self._sleep_loop()

    def stop(self):
        """停止睡眠管理器"""
        self._running = False
        self._wake_signal.set()

    def _sleep_loop(self):
        while self._running:
            if self._should_enter_sleep():
                self._enter_sleep()
            time.sleep(5)

    def force_wake(self):
        """强制唤醒"""
        self._wake_signal.set()

    def get_phase(self) -> str:
        return self._phase.value

    def is_sleeping(self) -> bool:
        return self._phase not in [SleepPhase.IDLE, SleepPhase.EXITING]

    # ==================== 睡眠周期各阶段 ====================

    def _enter_sleep(self):
        """进入睡眠周期"""
        with self._lock:
            self._phase = SleepPhase.ENTERING
            logger.info("🌙 进入睡眠状态 (时空编织模式)")
            self._cycle_stats = {"temporal_edges_added": 0, "spatial_edges_added": 0, "causal_patterns_found": 0, "hyperlinks_created": 0, "nodes_evolved": 0}

        try:
            # 1. 衰减情境层
            self._phase = SleepPhase.DECAY
            decay_count = self._decay_context()

            # 2. 时空编织：将情境层节点关联到时空图
            self._phase = SleepPhase.TEMPORAL_WEAVING
            temporal_added = self._weave_temporal()

            # 3. 空间聚类：将空间邻近的节点聚类
            self._phase = SleepPhase.SPATIAL_CLUSTERING
            spatial_added = self._cluster_spatial()

            # 4. 因果模式识别
            self._phase = SleepPhase.CAUSAL_RECOGNITION
            causal_found = self._recognize_causal_patterns()

            # 5. 超链接压缩
            self._phase = SleepPhase.HYPERLINK_COMPRESSION
            hyperlinks = self._compress_hyperlinks()

            # 6. 自演化
            self._phase = SleepPhase.AUTO_EVOLVE
            evolved = self._auto_evolve()

            # 统计
            self._cycle_stats["temporal_edges_added"] = temporal_added
            self._cycle_stats["spatial_edges_added"] = spatial_added
            self._cycle_stats["causal_patterns_found"] = causal_found
            self._cycle_stats["hyperlinks_created"] = hyperlinks
            self._cycle_stats["nodes_evolved"] = evolved

            logger.info(f"💤 睡眠周期完成: 衰减 {decay_count} 条, 时间边 {temporal_added}, 空间边 {spatial_added}, 因果模式 {causal_found}, 超链接 {hyperlinks}, 演化 {evolved}")

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ 睡眠状态异常: {e}", exc_info=True)

        finally:
            self._phase = SleepPhase.IDLE
            self._wake_signal.clear()

    # ---- 阶段1：衰减 ----

    def _decay_context(self) -> int:
        """衰减情境层"""
        if not self._memory_system:
            logger.warning("⚠️ 记忆系统未设置，跳过衰减")
            return 0

        try:
            count = self._memory_system.context_decay(self.config.decay_rate)
            logger.info(f"   📉 情境层衰减: {count} 条更新")
            return count
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"   ❌ 衰减失败: {e}")
            return 0

    # ---- 阶段2：时空编织 ----

    def _weave_temporal(self) -> int:
        """
        时空编织：将情境层节点按时间顺序关联到时空图
        从情境层提取最近的条目，按时间顺序建立时间边
        """
        if not self._memory_system:
            return 0

        try:
            context = self._memory_system._context
            if len(context) < 2:
                return 0

            # 取最近的条目
            recent = context[-self.config.temporal_window:]
            if len(recent) < 2:
                return 0

            # 按时间排序
            sorted_entries = sorted(recent, key=lambda x: x.get("timestamp", 0))
            added = 0

            # 为每对相邻条目建立时间边
            for i in range(len(sorted_entries) - 1):
                current = sorted_entries[i]
                next_entry = sorted_entries[i + 1]

                # 查找或创建对应的时空节点
                current_nodes = self._memory_system.spacetime.find_nodes_by_time(
                    current.get("timestamp", 0) - 0.1,
                    current.get("timestamp", 0) + 0.1
                )
                next_nodes = self._memory_system.spacetime.find_nodes_by_time(
                    next_entry.get("timestamp", 0) - 0.1,
                    next_entry.get("timestamp", 0) + 0.1
                )

                # 如果没有对应节点，跳过
                if not current_nodes or not next_nodes:
                    continue

                source = current_nodes[0].id
                target = next_nodes[0].id

                # 检查是否已存在时间边
                existing = False
                for edge in self._memory_system.spacetime.temporal_edges.values():
                    if edge.source == source and edge.target == target:
                        existing = True
                        break

                if not existing:
                    edge = self._memory_system.spacetime.add_temporal_edge(
                        source=source,
                        target=target,
                        relation_type="sequential",
                        confidence=0.5
                    )
                    added += 1

            if added > 0:
                logger.info(f"   ⏳ 时空编织: 添加 {added} 条时间边")
            return added

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"   ❌ 时空编织失败: {e}")
            return 0

    # ---- 阶段3：空间聚类 ----

    def _cluster_spatial(self) -> int:
        """
        空间聚类：将空间邻近的节点建立空间边
        基于时空坐标的距离
        """
        if not self._memory_system:
            return 0

        try:
            nodes = list(self._memory_system.spacetime.nodes.values())
            if len(nodes) < 2:
                return 0

            added = 0
            threshold = self.config.spatial_threshold

            for i, node1 in enumerate(nodes):
                if not node1.spatial_coordinates:
                    continue
                for j in range(i + 1, len(nodes)):
                    node2 = nodes[j]
                    if not node2.spatial_coordinates:
                        continue

                    # 计算空间距离
                    for modality in node1.spatial_coordinates:
                        if modality not in node2.spatial_coordinates:
                            continue
                        coords1 = node1.spatial_coordinates[modality]
                        coords2 = node2.spatial_coordinates[modality]
                        if len(coords1) != len(coords2):
                            continue

                        distance = sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(coords1, coords2)) ** 0.5
                        if distance < threshold:
                            # 检查是否已存在空间边
                            existing = False
                            for edge in self._memory_system.spacetime.spatial_edges.values():
                                if (edge.source == node1.id and edge.target == node2.id) or \
                                   (edge.source == node2.id and edge.target == node1.id):
                                    existing = True
                                    break

                            if not existing:
                                self._memory_system.spacetime.add_spatial_edge(
                                    source=node1.id,
                                    target=node2.id,
                                    relation_type="adjacent" if distance < threshold * 0.5 else "similar",
                                    spatial_distance=distance,
                                    confidence=1.0 - distance / threshold
                                )
                                added += 1

            if added > 0:
                logger.info(f"   📐 空间聚类: 添加 {added} 条空间边")
            return added

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"   ❌ 空间聚类失败: {e}")
            return 0

    # ---- 阶段4：因果模式识别 ----

    def _recognize_causal_patterns(self) -> int:
        """
        因果模式识别：发现重复出现的时空演化模式
        检查时间边，识别出重复出现且高置信度的模式
        """
        if not self._memory_system:
            return 0

        try:
            found = 0
            # 遍历所有时间边，检查重复模式
            edges_to_check = list(self._memory_system.spacetime.temporal_edges.values())

            for edge in edges_to_check:
                # 检查是否形成因果模式
                if edge.repeat_count >= self.config.min_causal_repeat and edge.confidence >= 0.5:
                    # 检查是否已有因果模式
                    existing = False
                    for pattern in self._memory_system.spacetime.causal_patterns.values():
                        if pattern.source_node_id == edge.source and pattern.target_node_id == edge.target:
                            existing = True
                            break

                    if not existing:
                        # 创建因果模式
                        pattern = self._memory_system.spacetime._detect_causal_pattern(
                            edge.source, edge.target, edge.id
                        )
                        found += 1

            if found > 0:
                logger.info(f"   🔗 因果模式识别: 发现 {found} 个新因果模式")
            return found

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"   ❌ 因果模式识别失败: {e}")
            return 0

    # ---- 阶段5：超链接压缩 ----

    def _compress_hyperlinks(self) -> int:
        """
        超链接压缩：将低重要性节点压缩为超链接
        """
        if not self._memory_system:
            return 0

        try:
            nodes = list(self._memory_system.spacetime.nodes.values())
            if not nodes:
                return 0

            # 筛选低重要性节点
            low_importance = [n for n in nodes if n.importance < self.config.importance_threshold_low and not n.is_compressed]
            if not low_importance:
                return 0

            # 限制数量
            to_compress = low_importance[:self.config.max_compress_per_cycle]
            compressed = 0

            for node in to_compress:
                hyper = self._memory_system.spacetime.compress_to_hyperlink(
                    [node.id],
                    f"压缩_{node.modality}_{node.id[:8]}"
                )
                if hyper:
                    compressed += 1

            if compressed > 0:
                logger.info(f"   🔗 超链接压缩: 压缩 {compressed} 个节点")
            return compressed

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"   ❌ 超链接压缩失败: {e}")
            return 0

    # ---- 阶段6：自演化 ----

    def _auto_evolve(self) -> int:
        """
        自演化：重要性驱动的图结构演化
        """
        if not self._memory_system:
            return 0

        try:
            before = len(self._memory_system.spacetime.nodes)
            self._memory_system.spacetime.auto_evolve(
                threshold_high=self.config.importance_threshold_high,
                threshold_low=self.config.importance_threshold_low
            )
            after = len(self._memory_system.spacetime.nodes)
            evolved = after - before
            if evolved > 0:
                logger.info(f"   🧬 自演化: 新增 {evolved} 个节点")
            return max(0, evolved)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"   ❌ 自演化失败: {e}")
            return 0

    # ==================== 手动触发 ====================

    def run_cycle(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """手动触发一个完整的睡眠周期"""
        logger.info("🔄 手动触发睡眠周期...")
        self._enter_sleep()
        return {
            "status": "completed",
            "stats": self._cycle_stats,
            "consecutive_empty": self._consecutive_empty_cycles
        }

    # ==================== 危机感知 ====================

    def _detect_crisis_patterns(self) -> None:
        """
        检测危机模式：识别重复出现的负面时空模式
        """
        if not self._memory_system:
            return

        try:
            nodes = list(self._memory_system.spacetime.nodes.values())
            for node in nodes:
                if node.is_causal_source and node.importance > 0.8:
                    # 高重要性因果源，检查是否有负面模式
                    content = str(node.content)
                    if "错误" in content or "失败" in content or "异常" in content:
                        pattern_id = f"crisis_{node.id}_{content[:20]}"
                        self._crisis_patterns["repetition_counts"][pattern_id] = \
                            self._crisis_patterns["repetition_counts"].get(pattern_id, 0) + 1

                        if self._crisis_patterns["repetition_counts"][pattern_id] >= self._crisis_threshold:
                            logger.warning(f"⚠️ 危机模式检测到: {content[:50]}...")
                            self._trigger_preventive_calibration(pattern_id, node)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"危机模式检测失败: {e}")

    def _trigger_preventive_calibration(self, pattern_id: str, node):
        """触发预防性校准"""
        try:
            if self._memory_system:
                self._memory_system.store(
                    "structure",
                    {
                        "type": "crisis_pattern",
                        "pattern_id": pattern_id,
                        "node_id": node.id,
                        "content": str(node.content)[:200],
                        "timestamp": time.time()
                    },
                    {"source": "sleep_crisis_detection", "severity": "warning"}
                )
            logger.info(f"🔔 预防性校准已触发: {pattern_id}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"预防性校准失败: {e}")

    # ==================== 信息 ====================

    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """获取睡眠管理器状态"""
        return {
            "phase": self._phase.value,
            "is_sleeping": self.is_sleeping(),
            "idle_duration": time.time() - self._last_interaction,
            "idle_timeout": self.config.idle_timeout,
            "cycle_stats": self._cycle_stats,
            "consecutive_empty": self._consecutive_empty_cycles
        }
```
